[1;38;5;78mThe three blind spots, and why two of   
them are this wave's own doing[0m          
                                        
The census has exactly one rot check, at
:585-602, and it tests EXISTENCE: a     
ledger row naming a file that is no     
longer on disk is an error. That catches
deletion. It cannot catch the three     
shapes that actually rot a price column.
                                        
[38;5;59m• [0mSTALE PRICE. No content hash, no date 
  arithmetic, no re-measurement. pds-   
  record-parity.test.sh will print      
  CPU=4.45s after someone doubles the   
  harness's work, forever, and nothing  
  reds.                                 
[38;5;59m• [0mSTALE DISPOSITION EVIDENCE. Five rows 
  cite "by run 2026-08-03: <cmd> rc=N". 
  The census never re-takes any of them.
  A disposition survives its own repair.
[38;5;59m• [0mORPHANED DISPOSITION. Read the cond at
  :519-540: legA=yes AND legB=true      
  short-circuits to THROUGH and the     
  ledger is NEVER consulted. A          
  disposition row for a now-THROUGH     
  instrument is silently unread - not an
  error, not a warning, not printed.    
                                        
[38;5;69m▌[0m THE COUPLING THAT MAKES THIS THE      
[38;5;69m▌[0m SPINE RATHER THAN A TANGENT: two of   
[38;5;69m▌[0m the three deferred slices the wish    
[38;5;69m▌[0m orders will make the census lie the   
[38;5;69m▌[0m day they land. pds-w44-hetzner-       
[38;5;69m▌[0m offline-door puts --selftest-offline  
[38;5;69m▌[0m through a rider plus                  
[38;5;69m▌[0m ELIXIR_TEST_ONLY_PATHS - legA/legB    
[38;5;69m▌[0m then compute THROUGH and the          
[38;5;69m▌[0m ENVIRONMENT row at :128 becomes an    
[38;5;69m▌[0m unflagged orphan asserting a refusal  
[38;5;69m▌[0m that is no longer the disposition.    
[38;5;69m▌[0m pds-w44-charter-sweep-adjudication    
[38;5;69m▌[0m takes the sweep from rc=1 to rc=0 -   
[38;5;69m▌[0m and CONTENT-RED, evidenced by a run   
[38;5;69m▌[0m nobody re-takes, outlives the red it  
[38;5;69m▌[0m names. Price provenance is a          
[38;5;69m▌[0m PREREQUISITE for the wish's own item  
[38;5;69m▌[0m 2, not an addition to it.             
                                        
[1;38;5;78mThe direction[0m                           
                                        
The epic's law has walked one rung per  
wave. A receipt lies when the value it  
emits does not descend from the write   
return. A class lies when it does not   
descend from its substitution (wave 42).
A gate lies when its green costs nothing
to produce (wave 43). Wave 45's sentence
is the one the new instrument earns:    
                                        
[38;5;69m▌[0m A PRICE LIES WHEN IT DOES NOT         
[38;5;69m▌[0m DESCEND FROM A METER THE INSTRUMENT   
[38;5;69m▌[0m ITSELF DROVE - AND A DISPOSITION      
[38;5;69m▌[0m LIES WHEN ITS EVIDENCE NAMES A RUN    
[38;5;69m▌[0m THE INSTRUMENT NEVER RE-TAKES.        
                                        
What the finished wave looks like:      
./scripts/pds-door-census.sh still exits
0 on main and still costs seconds - it  
is a door, and its own 3.32 s row is its
own gate cost, so any design that prices
it out of its own door is disqualified  
before it is written. But every number  
it prints is now either taken by a meter
it drove in THIS run, or a cached       
measurement carrying the content hash of
the instrument it prices, so that a     
changed instrument turns the row PRICE- 
STALE and reds. A --measure verb takes  
the measurement and emits the exact     
ledger row, so re-pricing is one command
rather than a hand ritual - the number  
descends from a meter the instrument    
drove, and anyone can re-take it.       
Dispositions whose evidence is a RUN get
re-run under an opt-in, threshold-      
printed tier, so CONTENT-RED cannot     
survive its own repair. And an orphaned 
ledger row is an ERROR, because that is 
the exact shape both of this wave's own 
slices create.                          
                                        
Around that spine, the debts get paid at
the new base. Priority order, and the   
reasoning behind it:

[1;38;5;78mThe direction debate[0m                    
                                        
Three directions were developed far     
enough to cost something, then argued   
against each other.                     
                                        
[1;38;5;59mRival A - pay the debts, period[0m         
                                        
Do exactly the wish's items 1 through 5:
three deferred slices, two owed reviews,
the lv_verdict half, the criterion      
lesson. Do not touch the census - it    
exits 0, wave 44 declared it done, and  
reopening it is a fifth wave of arguing 
about a column. This is the disciplined 
reading of the brief and it has a real  
claim on the wave.                      
                                        
WHY IT LOSES: it hands the wave a false 
floor. Two of its own three slices      
silently corrupt the instrument, so "pay
the debts" manufactures the next wave's 
finding. And it violates the mandate the
user restates every single wave -       
DERIVE, NEVER TRANSCRIBE, INCLUDING FROM
THIS BRIEF - by inheriting "the price   
column is an instrument now" as a       
premise instead of running it. I ran it.
It is half an instrument, and the half  
that is prose is the half the column is 
named after.                            
                                        
[1;38;5;59mRival B - measure every price, every run[0m
                                        
The pure fix: no cache, no ledger, no   
staleness possible. The census drives   
the meter for every row on every        
invocation and the price column is a    
measurement by construction.            
                                        
WHY IT LOSES, on the epic's own         
evidence: D648 proved wall is not a     
property of a door on a shared host - a 
fixed workload's wall spanned 5.8x at   
constant load while its user CPU spanned
27 percent. A re-measuring census       
becomes the most expensive program in   
its own denominator: 40.33 s for pds-   
ledger-census_test.sh alone, plus the   
receipt census whose expensive arm D646 
showed conceals ~140 s minimum from any 
outer meter. It would also have to price
ITSELF - a fixpoint. Worst of all it    
converts a 4 s door into an ungateable  
one, killing the census's own THROUGH   
row, which is the single most self-     
defeating outcome available to this     
wave. The honest design is not "always  
measure" but "never assert a measurement
you cannot re-take, and know when it    
expired."                               
                                        
[1;38;5;59mRival C - spend the column, gate the    
cheap doors[0m                             
                                        
Take the census output at face value and
do the obvious thing the wish's title   
demands - SPEND IT. Put every affordable
row through the required gate: hetzner  
offline at 2.15 s, pds-record-          
parity.test.sh at 4.45 s, pds-scratch-  
target_test.sh at 8.91 s. Maximum       
visible progress, and it is exactly what
four waves of arguing were FOR.         
                                        
WHY IT LOSES AS A WHOLE DIRECTION, and  
why it is absorbed as a part: gating on 
unverified prices is D633's sin at one  
remove. pds-scratch-target_test.sh's own
shipped row says "Hermeticity on a      
runner WITHOUT local Postgres is        
unproven here" - gating it is a day-one 
red on a foreign runner, the identical  
trap wave 44 documented for sheet-grid- 
js.yml. Rival C survives as exactly ONE 
new door: the one whose price and green 
I measured myself today.                
                                        
[1;38;5;59mWhy the winner won[0m                      
                                        
The spine plus the debts, with hetzner- 
offline as the one new door, is the only
direction in which the wish's own item 2
does not manufacture the next wave's    
finding. It applies the epic's law to   
the epic's newest artifact, which is    
what every wave of this epic has        
actually been about. And it converts    
"spend the column" from a slogan into   
something that is safe to do more than  
once.                                   
                                        
[1;38;5;59mThe sharpest attack on the winner, and  
how the wave absorbs it[0m                 
                                        
[38;5;69m▌[0m THE ATTACK: "You are reopening a      
[38;5;69m▌[0m slice that shipped 11/12 with run     
[38;5;69m▌[0m evidence and rc=0 on main, on a       
[38;5;69m▌[0m distinction - 'measured' versus       
[38;5;69m▌[0m 'cached with provenance' - that is    
[38;5;69m▌[0m philosophy, not a defect. Nobody is   
[38;5;69m▌[0m harmed by a three-day-old CPU         
[38;5;69m▌[0m figure. This is the fifth             
[38;5;69m▌[0m consecutive wave arguing about a      
[38;5;69m▌[0m column, which is precisely what wave  
[38;5;69m▌[0m 44 ended."                            
                                        
That attack has real force, and the wave
absorbs it by refusing to argue and     
making it a RUN. The spine ships only if
the verify round can demonstrate, by    
mutation against a real tree, a case    
where the shipped census prints a number
or a class the tree contradicts. I      
already hold one candidate - the hetzner
row's wrong-arm evidence - and this     
wave's own slices manufacture two more. 
IF THE VERIFIERS CANNOT MAKE THE SHIPPED
CENSUS PRINT A FALSEHOOD AGAINST A REAL 
TREE, THE SPINE IS CUT AND THIS WAVE    
BECOMES RIVAL A. That is the fail-before
condition, declared before the fleet    
flies, and it is the honest version of  
"derive, never transcribe" turned on my 
own direction.                          
                                        
Second-order absorption, as a criterion 
rather than a hope: the design must not 
balloon the census's own price. A       
content hash per row is one shasum      
across 20 files - milliseconds. Re-     
running cheap dispositions is opt-in and
threshold-printed, never on --check's   
default path.                           
                                        
[1;38;5;78mStanding hazards this wave carries[0m      
                                        
[38;5;59m• [0mTHE FORMATTER VERSION TRAP, newly     
  measured in wave 44: CI pins Elixir   
  1.18.1; this host runs 1.19.5. #9380  
  reddened Format while mix format --   
  check-formatted exited 0 locally on   
  the SAME commit. Apply CI's own       
  suggested output verbatim and verify  
  BOTH formatters accept it, or the red 
  only moves.                           
[38;5;59m• [0mOVERLAP RISK, declared: #9380 now owns
  scripts/elixir-path-escape-check.sh   
  and the hetzner slice edits the same  
  declared set. Cloud Console wave 28   
  runs concurrently and owns            
  .github/workflows/**. Coordinate      
  before touching either.               
[38;5;59m• [0mBOTH LEGS OR NEITHER on the hetzner   
  slice - the instrument AND its        
  ELIXIR_TEST_ONLY_PATHS entry, or it is
  wave 42's #9290/#9292 a third time.   
[38;5;59m• [0mThe primary checkout is stale at      
  a31faa52d and dirty across eight      
  charter files. Read the charter via   
  git show origin/main:<path>; build in 
  worktrees only.                       
[38;5;59m• [0mA slice PR citing a D-number must not 
  merge before the charter PR that      
  defines it - the D643 rule, bought at 
  the cost of 13 shipped citations      
  pointing at decisions main did not    
  define.                               
[38;5;59m• [0mPUSH EARLY AND OPEN THE PR ON THE     
  FIRST GREEN COMMIT. Wave 43 lost four 
  branches because nothing was pushed   
  until review, and review never ran.   
                                        
[1;38;5;78mThe survey plan[0m                         
                                        
Fifteen Opus surveyors at medium effort.
Width now buys precision later: every   
load-bearing assumption in the direction
above is a question below, and the first
three are aimed squarely at the spine's 
own fail-before condition.

[1;38;5;78mWhere the reports contradict the        
direction, and each other[0m               
                                        
[38;5;59m• [0mTHE DIRECTION SAID '3 of 19'. The wish
  said it too. BOTH ARE DEAD: the census
  enters its own denominator by design  
  and prints 20 with 4 THROUGH.         
  Confirmed by two independent runs.    
  This is the ELEVENTH consecutive wave 
  in which the dispatch is refuted from 
  the artifact - and this time the      
  direction refuted itself before the   
  survey did.                           
[38;5;59m• [0mTHE DIRECTION SAID the census costs   
  2.80 s CPU / 4.13 s wall; a surveyor  
  measured 2.44 s CPU / 3.47 s wall;    
  both under load1 12-19. The FINDING is
  unaffected (the gap to 40.33 s only   
  widens) but neither figure is quotable
  as the code's price. Every number in  
  this wave was taken on a host at load1
  12-33. NOTHING here may enter a ledger
  row without a quiet-host re-take.     
[38;5;59m• [0mTHE LEDGER-CENSUS PRICE IS STALE-HIGH,
  not stale-low: the row says 40.33 s   
  CPU / 68.24 s wall; a re-measure at   
  load1 33 got 32.22 s / 42.61 s. So the
  price column is wrong in BOTH         
  directions across different rows. A   
  staleness arm that only catches growth
  catches half the defect.              
[38;5;59m• [0mTHE HETZNER SLICE'S DECLARED FILE SET 
  IS TOO NARROW. The bp task declares   
  only the rider + elixir-path-escape-  
  check.sh. Landing exactly those two   
  produces a green census carrying a    
  FALSE ENVIRONMENT row AND             
  price=UNMEASURED-LOCAL on the door it 
  just measured three times. BOTH LEGS  
  OR NEITHER must be widened to FOUR    
  files.                                
[38;5;59m• [0mTHE LADDER'S NUMBERS SURVIVE THE NEW  
  BASE (252 population, 74 judged-      
  coverage, PROVEN-BACKED 23 with       
  |A|=20, |B|=3) - but OVERLAP IS 0, so 
  leg_a + leg_b and                     
  Enum.count(MapSet.union(...)) BOTH    
  PRINT 23. A builder can ship the      
  forbidden addition, satisfy every     
  number-quoting criterion, and pass.   
  The injection proof is the ONLY       
  discriminator that exists at this     
  base.                                 
[38;5;59m• [0mTHE CHARTER SWEEP IS THE LAST SLICE,  
  NOT A FREE-STANDING ONE. Its          
  fingerprint hashes the whole          
  normalized charter line, so           
  adjudicating before the WAVE-45       
  charter merges orphans every row it   
  writes - and two of the 59 arrivals   
  are self-referential (its own dispatch
  row at charter:12685, and the         
  adjudication table file itself at     
  charter:2782).

[1;38;5;145mHIGH-FLIP-RISK, declared. [0mSlice 1 —     
whether a Code.compile_string           
substitution proves what a tautology did
not, on a LIVE authorization door, and  
whether the repair stays out of api/lib 
and therefore out of Console's fence.   
Slice 2 — whether the argument-list     
predicate still admits a fraudulent     
THROUGH, and whether it now DECLINES an 
honest door; the fix must be proven in  
BOTH directions and the Port.open arm is
the second-direction oracle. Slice 6 —  
whether the retire shape is a bypass,   
and whether the orphan check has a      
false-positive mode. All three warrant a
genuinely INDEPENDENT second reviewer   
before merge; this workflow spawns one  
reviewer, so that dispatch is the lead's
manual step.

the lead dispatches each after its      
dependency MERGES. A round-1 slice whose
dependency is unmerged burns a builder  
to produce a BLOCKED report.

WHAT STALLED — nothing. Three slices    
were deferred by the sequenced-rounds   
law, each on a hard dependency that has 
to merge first: pds-w45-census-ledger-  
integrity (round 2, after #9434 — same  
file, ~180 lines move), pds-w45-price-  
provenance (round 3, after round 2 —    
same file, built on top), pds-w44-      
hetzner-offline-door (round 4, after    
both — it needs the retire shape and -- 
measure those rounds create). Their     
tasks are open, unclaimed and 0/N met,  
which is the honest state, not a stall. 
                                        
[1;38;5;78mTHE TWO FINDINGS REVIEW ADDED, AND THE  
RULES THEY BUY[0m                          
                                        
PDS-D664 — A REPAIRED PREDICATE CARRIES 
ITS OLD DEFECT FOR EXACTLY ONE LINE.    
arg_span replaced the three-line        
proximity window with a real paren-     
balance walk, and the three fraud shapes
flipped as designed. It still returned  
the whole CLOSING LINE, so              
"System.cmd(...) ;                      
File.regular?(@fraud)" — a token named  
after the closing paren, on the call's  
own line — still read as argument-list  
membership: bound, never executed,      
THROUGH with a price. One line of the   
old window living inside the new        
predicate, past a 13-arm green selftest.
THE RULE: when you replace a window with
a boundary, the boundary is where the   
window's last residue hides — test the  
token that sits just OUTSIDE the new    
edge, not only the ones that sat inside 
the old one.                            
                                        
PDS-D665 — BOTH LEGS OR NEITHER APPLIES 
TO EVIDENCE STRINGS, NOT ONLY TO        
DECLARED PATH SETS. The wish states the 
both-legs law for the hetzner slice's   
ELIXIR_TEST_ONLY_PATHS entry. It bit    
somewhere nobody named: the sweep's own 
fix makes --selftest rc=0, which turns  
the door census row whose evidence      
quotes "--selftest rc=1" into a stale   
receipt the moment it merges — on the   
instrument whose entire subject is      
receipts that lie. The builder was      
fenced out of the census and correctly  
recorded the correction in two durable  
venues rather than reaching across the  
fence. Review co-landed the one-line row
edit on the SAME branch: a behaviour and
the receipt describing it merge together
or not at all. A fence that splits those
two has to be closed by whoever holds   
both, and the fix belongs on the branch 
that causes the drift.

work, stamped mid-claim with real       
evidence rather than batched at the end,
and left in_progress with only the      
merge-gated row open — with one         
exception, now fixed. Tasks outside this
wave were not touched.

[1;38;5;78mGRADE — A[0m                               
                                        
WHAT EARNS IT. The wave paid all three  
items the wish named and paid them in   
the order the dependencies allow. The   
second-review debt on #9377 is now      
discharged BY THE MERGING ARTIFACT      
rather than by a reviewer's hand        
mutation — that was the live gap on     
merged code, and it is the difference   
between "someone proved it once" and    
"the repo proves it every run". The     
ladder slice is the wave's best work: it
recognised that its own headline number 
is evidence of nothing, said so in the  
printed output, and shipped the only    
discriminator that exists as a selftest 
case verified in both directions. The   
sweep slice reproduced its fail-open    
with the CORRECT oracle after the       
criterion named the wrong one, and      
closed it with the discriminator that   
was already on the wire. Four of five   
slices refuted something in their own   
dispatch by run — the eleventh, twelfth 
and thirteenth consecutive refutations —
and each wrote the correction into the  
artifact instead of quietly satisfying a
figure that does not exist.             
                                        
WHAT KEEPS IT OFF A+. Two of the five   
needed a reviewer fix to a predicate    
that had just been tightened, and in    
both cases the defect was one step past 
where the builder stopped looking: the  
first occurrence rather than the only   
occurrence, the closing line rather than
the closing paren. Both slices had      
green, honest, non-vacuous selftests    
through the defect. The wave also left  
three real UNPROVEN arms shipping green 
by print rather than by mutation — the  
ladder's :stale filter, arg_span's      
"every call opening on the line" clause,
and its 40-line bound — each filed, none
closed, and each is exactly the shape   
this epic exists to refuse. And every   
price and timing in this wave is LOCAL: 
no PDS door has yet run on a foreign    
runner, so the price column remains an  
instrument calibrated against one host. 
                                        
NOT A CRITICISM, ON THE RECORD: the     
wish's item 4 (the unbuilt "?" variable-
bound decline) was RULED OUT with a     
reason, not quietly dropped — PDS-D661 —
and the ladder slice printed            
declined_live=0 as a BOUND on the       
sentence instead, derived THROUGH       
lvs_joinable?/1 so the number cannot    
drift from the predicate it describes.  
Ruling it out was the right half of     
"finish it or rule it out".             
                                        
[1;38;5;78mWHAT THE NEXT WAVE TAKES[0m                
                                        
MERGE ORDER FIRST. The five round-1 PRs 
are file-disjoint except that #9434 and 
#9441 both touch scripts/pds-door-      
census.sh — #9434 in the classifier and 
the selftest, #9441 at line 123 of      
PDS_DOOR_DISPOSITIONS only — so either  
order merges without a conflict. Then   
dispatch the deferred slices STRICTLY in
dependency order, re-deriving every     
anchor at each merged base, because each
one moves the file the next one reads.
